16,044 views  7 Feb 2026  #AIAppBuilder #NextJS #Base44
In this video, We are building *ForgeAI* — a *production-ready, full-stack AI app builder* that works exactly like Base44/Lovable/Bolt/Replit, but built from scratch using a modern AI-first stack.

You’ll build something that can create a website and app from *a single prompt or design screenshot* to a fully functional, deployable web application — complete with live previews, editable code, background jobs, and AI agents.

This is **not a toy project**.
This is a *complete playbook* for building real AI SaaS products.

---

🚀 What You’ll See in This Video

Prompt-to-app generation (SaaS landing page + Airbnb-style clone)
Live preview without local setup
AI-generated production-grade code
Inline code editing using Monaco (VS Code engine)
Image-to-code using design screenshots
Feature gating with Free vs Pro subscriptions
Background jobs and AI agents running securely in the cloud
Full production deployment mindset

---

🧠 Built with a Modern AI-First Stack

ForgeAI is built using **cutting-edge production tooling**:

Core Stack
🚀 *Next.js 16 + React 19*
🎨 *Tailwind CSS v4 + Shadcn/ui*
📡 *Elysia.js* for end-to-end type safety
🔁 *Inngest* for background jobs & workflows
🧠 *Inngest Agent Kit* for AI orchestration
🔐 *Clerk* for authentication & billing
📦 *Prisma + Neon (PostgreSQL)* for database integration

AI & Execution
🧠 **AI model support**: OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini
🖥️ *E2B Cloud Sandboxes* for secure runtime execution
🧱 *Component & full app generation from prompts*
🗂️ *Live project preview with shareable URLs*
🧪 *Preview ↔ Code Explorer toggle*
✏️ *Inline code editing*
🖼️ *Images powered by Unsplash*

---

🎯 It will help you understand

How tools like Base44 and Lovable actually work internally — and how *you can build your own AI app builder* that’s:

Production-ready
Monetizable
Scalable
Deployable to real customers

If you want to build **AI SaaS products**, **developer tools**, or **AI website builders**, this video gives you the full blueprint.
